  1. HARD RULES                                                                 
  ==================================================                            
                                                                                
  :Phase نیا رد                                                                 
                                                                                
  .سیونن یدک چیه -                                                              
  .نکن ارجا یدک چیه -                                                           
  .نکن ارجا یشیامزآ چیه -                                                       
  .نکن یسیونزاب ار یشهوژپ لیاف چیه -

  - Algorithm نکن ضوع ار.                                                       
  - Dependency نکن بصن دیدج.                                                    
  .دوش هداد هزاجا prompt نیا یاهتنا رد اًتحارص هکنیا رگم ،نکن داجیا دیدج لیاف -  
   لفق »Decision« ای تسا »Recommendation« هکنیا ندرکص‌خشم نودب ار Decision چیه - 
  .نکن

  exclude the solutions of water overconsumption in the tanks». سپ F7 زا
  Recommendation هب Evidence-backed دبایی‌م اقترا.

   :Negm .تسا سایقم هب هتسباو — دش لح »گنردالب لرتنک« یاعدا رد یرهاظ ضراعت کی .۸

  per prediction) on a DMA level. However, due to the complexity of whole water

  Proposal («یریگم‌یمصت نامز») سایقم رد Net3 (۹۲ طسوتم ،هرگ) تسا عافد لباق —

  ۹.۴ Hydraulic model — LOCKED

  PDA (Wagner et al. 1988) ود ره ،یبایزرا و شزومآ یارب.

  $$d_{j,t} = \begin{cases} 0 & p_{j,t} \le P_0\
  D_{j,t}\left(\frac{p_{j,t}-P_0}{P_f-P_0}\right)^{1/e} & P_0 < p_{j,t} < P_f\
  D_{j,t} & p_{j,t} \ge P_f\end{cases} \qquad \text{DSR} = \frac{\sum_{t,j}
  d_{j,t}}{\sum_{t,j} D_{j,t}}$$

  P₀ = 0، 1/e = 0.5. [UNRESOLVED — A5/G7] P_f, p^min_service, p^max. لکتورپ 
  LOCKED: عجرم هطقن p^min = 20 m، P_f زا رتالاب یمک p^min (یئزج لالقتسا ظفح DSR
   عیزوت لیلحت زا سپ p^max ؛}۲۵ ،۲۰ ،۱۴ ،۱۰{ یور یرابجا S3 تیساسح ،)ضقن شرامش زا
  .۰ ماگ رد راشف

  ۹.۷ Action — LOCKED

  $$s^{\text{PRV}}{v,t} = \mathrm{clip}\big(\tilde{s}{v,t},\

  \mathrm{clip}\big(\tilde{\omega}{u,t},\ \omega{u,t-1} \pm \delta_u\big)$$

  s^PRV_v ∈ [p^min_service, s̄_v] (رتم ،فده یجورخ ده)؛ ω_u ∈ [0.7, 1.0] (تبسن
   شور( یفداصت rollout ۱٬۰۰۰ زا PRV یدورو راشف رثکادح = s̄_v .)یکیناکم دیق ،رود

  ۹.۸ Reward — LOCKED

  $$r_t = -\big[, w_L \tilde{L}_t + w_V \tilde{V}_t + w_S \tilde{S}_t + w_E
  \tilde{E}_t ,\big]$$

  $$\tilde{L}t = \frac{\sum_j 
  Q^{\text{leak}}{j,t}}{L^{\text{ref}}{\text{step}}}, \qquad \tilde{V}t = 
  \frac{1}{|\mathcal{J}|}\sum_j\left(\frac{\nu{j,t}}{\nu^{\text{scale}}}\right)^
  {\beta}, \qquad \nu{j,t} = \max(0, p^{\min}!-!p_{j,t}) + \max(0,
  p_{j,t}!-!p^{\max})\ [\mathrm{m}]$$

  $$\tilde{S}t = 
  \frac{1}{|\mathcal{V}|+|\mathcal{U}|}\left[\sum_v\left(\frac{|\Delta 
  s{v,t}|}{\delta_v}\right)^2 +
  \sum_u\left(\frac{|\Delta\omega_{u,t}|}{\delta_u}\right)^2\right], \qquad
  \tilde{E}_t = \frac{P^{\text{pump}}t \Delta t}{E^{\text{ref}}{\text{step}}},\
  \eta = 0.75$$

   نیب یتوافتی‌ب عفر( رتم بسح رب هتسویپ ضقن :Negm مرف ربارب رد یراتخاس حالصا هس
  ۹٫۹ m ۲ و m)، هن قلطم مرف ،)یاه‌رگ فیرحت عفر( عجرم یویرانس اب یرسارس یزاسل‌امرن
  اهل‌حه‌ار :Hu دهاش( قرب هفرعت یزاسل‌دم نودب .)اهbaseline اب comparability( یلضافت
   ≥ H_tank,fin :نزخم تشگزاب تخس دیق .)هفرعت هن دناه‌تسباو فرصم یوگلا هب
  H_tank,ini (Hu ۱۷ هلداعم).

  ✅ READY TO LOCK — ۲۶ دروم

  PDA یاجه‌ب DDA · یلخاد تشن لدم در WNTR · تشن لدم emitter N₁=1.18 + نویساربیلاک
  · EPANET 2.2 (A1) · WNTR + EpanetSimulator (A2) · Python 3.12 (A6) · اضاقت% هب

   زا نارک ،Action absolute + rate-limit، VSP [0.7,1.0] · )C3( وگلا تسکش نومزآ

   لکتورپ · CO₂ (E6) فذح · )E7( هفرعت نودب ،η=0.75 اب یژرنا هلمج · )قلطم ،یرسارس
   · SAC comparator · میژر دیق + هدشظ‌فح Option C · PPO در · Pareto (E5) ههبج
   )G3( هتفایش‌هاک بوراج راکهار · )G2( طورشم هدعاق + ۲M timestep · ۵ seed–۱ هجدوب
   Hu تیعطقم‌دع لکتورپ · validation (G5) و فقوت لکتورپ · )G4( میظنت/هجدوب فاصنا ·
  + Δ هتشاده‌گن + OOD وگلا (H1–H3) · ۵ · ۲+۱ حطس میمعت baseline فذح + یرابجا MPC
   یدنبه‌بتر هدعاق + اهرایعم · )I3( رسه‌برس هطقن اب یتابساحم فاصنا لکتورپ ·
  feasibility-first (یرابجا تبث-شیپ) · یتعاس شرازگ + یرامآ لکتورپ · Ablation
  (Tier 1 اب A6 هتفایاقترا) · اب یرابجا تیساسح S9 لکتورپ · هتفایاقترا
   safety ،۳ حطس در · )J4( میمعت هناراکه‌ظفاحم یدنبه‌ژاو · M1–M4 یریذپدیلوتزاب
  filter، یناریا هکبش، MADDPG، ییامناج یزاسه‌نیهب · N1، N2، N4–N7 · هدعاق« حالصا

❯ ============================================================                  
  PHASE 3 — DECISION REVIEW, METHODOLOGY LOCK & IMPLEMENTATION GATE             
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  ROLE                                                                          
  You are the research and engineering partner for my Master's thesis.          
                                                                                
  This is NOT a new research project.                                           
  This is a direct continuation of:                                             
                                                                                
  - Phase 1 — Audit                                                             
  - Phase 1.5 — Deep Literature Review                                          
  - Phase 1.5-B — Evidence Completion & Verification                            
  - Phase 2 — Methodology Review                                                
                                                                                
  These phases have already been completed.                                     
                                                                                
  Your job now is to convert the existing evidence into:                        
  1. a rigorous Decision Register,                                              
  2. explicit human decisions,                                                  
  3. an internally consistent methodology specification,                        
  4. and a final Implementation Readiness Gate.                                 
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  0. ABSOLUTE CONTINUITY RULE                                                   
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  DO NOT restart the research from scratch.                                     
                                                                                
  DO NOT repeat the full 42-paper literature review.                            
                                                                                
  DO NOT re-read every PDF unless a specific unresolved decision requires       
  direct verification.                                                          
                                                                                
  DO NOT reconstruct old findings from memory if a transcript or prior          
  phase output is available on disk.                                            
                                                                                
  First inspect the current repository and available previous-session           
  transcripts/reports.                                                          
                                                                                
  Use the previous Phase 1/1.5/1.5-B/2 work as the starting point.              
                                                                                
  If previous reports are not persisted in docs/, search for the prior          
  session transcript(s) and recover the relevant prior conclusions.             
                                                                                
  If any previous conclusion cannot be recovered reliably:                      
  mark it as [NOT RECOVERED]                                                    
  instead of inventing it.                                                      
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  1. FIRST ACTION — ORIENTATION                                                 
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  Before making any decision:                                                   
                                                                                
  1. Read CLAUDE.md.                                                            
  2. Inspect git status.                                                        
  3. Inspect the current repository tree.                                       
  4. Identify all available prior-phase artifacts.                              
  5. Locate the previous session transcript if necessary.                       
  6. Determine which decisions are already locked and which remain open.        
  7. Produce a short Resume Check.                                              
                                                                                
  The Resume Check must contain:                                                
                                                                                
  - Previous phases completed                                                   
  - Last known completed section                                                
  - Current phase                                                               
  - Available evidence                                                          
  - Missing evidence                                                            
  - Locked decisions                                                            
  - Unresolved decisions                                                        
  - External verification limitations                                           
  - Current git status                                                          
  - Whether any files have changed                                              
                                                                                
  DO NOT begin methodology redesign before this orientation is complete.        
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  2. HARD RULES                                                                 
  =================                                                             
  ──── (1061 lines hidden) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── 
  an input should appear here.                                                  
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  34. HUMAN DECISION PROTECTION                                                 
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  Very important:                                                               
                                                                                
  You are not the final decision-maker.                                         
                                                                                
  Your role:                                                                    
                                                                                
  Evidence → analysis → recommendation.                                         
                                                                                
  My role:                                                                      
                                                                                
  researcher decision.                                                          
                                                                                
  Advisor's role:                                                               
                                                                                
  advisor approval where necessary.                                             
                                                                                
  Do not hide uncertainty by choosing the easiest implementation.               
                                                                                
  Do not optimize the methodology merely for convenience.                       
                                                                                
  Prefer scientific defensibility over coding convenience.                      
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  35. NO IMPLEMENTATION YET                                                     
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  Even if the methodology appears ready:                                        
                                                                                
  STOP AFTER THE REPORT.                                                        
                                                                                
  Do not:                                                                       
                                                                                
  - create Python files                                                         
  - create environment classes                                                  
  - run EPANET                                                                  
  - run PPO                                                                     
  - run SAC                                                                     
  - run experiments                                                             
  - create result files                                                         
  - install packages                                                            
                                                                                
  The purpose of this phase is methodological lock,                             
  not implementation.                                                           
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  36. RESUME / INTERRUPTION PROTOCOL                                            
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  If interrupted for any reason:                                                
                                                                                
  Before stopping, output:                                                      
                                                                                
  Resume Check                                                                  
                                                                                
  - Last completed section                                                      
  - Current section                                                             
  - Remaining sections                                                          
  - Decisions already locked                                                    
  - Decisions still open                                                        
  - Evidence already verified                                                   
  - Evidence still missing                                                      
  - External tools unavailable                                                  
  - Files changed                                                               
  - Files unchanged                                                             
                                                                                
  Never restart automatically after interruption.                               
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  37. FINAL PRINCIPLE                                                           
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  This phase must transform:                                                    
                                                                                
  Literature                                                                    
  ↓                                                                             
  Evidence                                                                      
  ↓                                                                             
  Scientific reasoning                                                          
  ↓                                                                             
  Decision Register                                                             
  ↓                                                                             
  Human approval                                                                
  ↓                                                                             
  Locked methodology                                                            
  ↓                                                                             
  Implementation gate                                                           
                                                                                
  Do not skip the human-decision layer.                                         
                                                                                
  Do not silently change the approved thesis.                                   
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "recommended" with "approved".                                 
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "not found" with "does not exist".                             
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "works in simulation" with "works in reality".                 
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "lower leakage" with "better system performance".              
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "PPO is chosen" with "PPO is proven superior".                 
                                                                                
  The final output should tell me, in a very unambiguous way:                   
                                                                                
  1. What is already decided?                                                   
  2. What should I decide?                                                      
  3. What should I ask my advisor?                                              
  4. What still needs evidence?                                                 
  5. What methodology is actually ready?                                        
  6. Exactly what blocks implementation?                                        
                                                                                
  END PHASE 3                                                                   
  ============================================================

  - Unresolved decisions                                                        
  - External verification limitations                                           
  - Current git status                                                          
  - Whether any files have changed                                              
                                                                                
  DO NOT begin methodology redesign before this orientation is complete.        
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  2. HARD RULES                                                                 
  =================                                                             
  ──── (1061 lines hidden) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── 
  an input should appear here.                                                  
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  34. HUMAN DECISION PROTECTION                                                 
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  Very important:                                                               
                                                                                
  You are not the final decision-maker.                                         
                                                                                
  Your role:                                                                    
                                                                                
  Evidence → analysis → recommendation.                                         
                                                                                
  My role:                                                                      
                                                                                
  researcher decision.                                                          
                                                                                
  Advisor's role:                                                               
                                                                                
  advisor approval where necessary.                                             
                                                                                
  Do not hide uncertainty by choosing the easiest implementation.               
                                                                                
  Do not optimize the methodology merely for convenience.                       
                                                                                
  Prefer scientific defensibility over coding convenience.                      
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  35. NO IMPLEMENTATION YET                                                     
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  Even if the methodology appears ready:                                        
                                                                                
  STOP AFTER THE REPORT.                                                        
                                                                                
  Do not:                                                                       
                                                                                
  - create Python files                                                         
  - create environment classes                                                  
  - run EPANET                                                                  
  - run PPO                                                                     
  - run SAC                                                                     
  - run experiments                                                             
  - create result files                                                         
  - install packages                                                            
                                                                                
  The purpose of this phase is methodological lock,                             
  not implementation.                                                           
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  36. RESUME / INTERRUPTION PROTOCOL                                            
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  If interrupted for any reason:                                                
                                                                                
  Before stopping, output:                                                      
                                                                                
  Resume Check                                                                  
                                                                                
  - Last completed section                                                      
  - Current section                                                             
  - Remaining sections                                                          
  - Decisions already locked                                                    
  - Decisions still open                                                        
  - Evidence already verified                                                   
  - Evidence still missing                                                      
  - External tools unavailable                                                  
  - Files changed                                                               
  - Files unchanged                                                             
                                                                                
  Never restart automatically after interruption.                               
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  37. FINAL PRINCIPLE                                                           
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  This phase must transform:                                                    
                                                                                
  Literature                                                                    
  ↓                                                                             
  Evidence                                                                      
  ↓                                                                             
  Scientific reasoning                                                          
  ↓                                                                             
  Decision Register                                                             
  ↓                                                                             
  Human approval                                                                
  ↓                                                                             
  Locked methodology                                                            
  ↓                                                                             
  Implementation gate                                                           
                                                                                
  Do not skip the human-decision layer.                                         
                                                                                
  Do not silently change the approved thesis.                                   
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "recommended" with "approved".                                 
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "not found" with "does not exist".                             
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "works in simulation" with "works in reality".                 
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "lower leakage" with "better system performance".              
                                                                                
  Do not confuse "PPO is chosen" with "PPO is proven superior".                 
                                                                                
  The final output should tell me, in a very unambiguous way:                   
                                                                                
  1. What is already decided?                                                   
  2. What should I decide?                                                      
  3. What should I ask my advisor?                                              
  4. What still needs evidence?                                                 
  5. What methodology is actually ready?                                        
  6. Exactly what blocks implementation?                                        
                                                                                
  END PHASE 3                                                                   
  ============================================================

  (request id: 202608290652535718272347qbpdgnr6LPCg)

❯ ============================================================                  
  PHASE 3 — DECISION REVIEW, METHODOLOGY LOCK & IMPLEMENTATION GATE             
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  ROLE                                                                          
  You are the research and engineering partner for my Master's thesis.          
                                                                                
  This is NOT a new research project.                                           
  This is a direct continuation of:                                             
                                                                                
  - Phase 1 — Audit                                                             
  - Phase 1.5 — Deep Literature Review                                          
  - Phase 1.5-B — Evidence Completion & Verification                            
  - Phase 2 — Methodology Review                                                
                                                                                
  These phases have already been completed.                                     
                                                                                
  Your job now is to convert the existing evidence into:                        
  1. a rigorous Decision Register,                                              
  2. explicit human decisions,                                                  
  3. an internally consistent methodology specification,                        
  4. and a final Implementation Readiness Gate.                                 
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  0. ABSOLUTE CONTINUITY RULE                                                   
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  DO NOT restart the research from scratch.                                     
                                                                                
  DO NOT repeat the full 42-paper literature review.                            
                                                                                
  DO NOT re-read every PDF unless a specific unresolved decision requires       
  direct verification.                                                          
                                                                                
  DO NOT reconstruct old findings from memory if a transcript or prior          
  phase output is available on disk.                                            
                                                                                
  First inspect the current repository and available previous-session           
  transcripts/reports.                                                          
                                                                                
  Use the previous Phase 1/1.5/1.5-B/2 work as the starting point.              
                                                                                
  If previous reports are not persisted in docs/, search for the prior          
  session transcript(s) and recover the relevant prior conclusions.             
                                                                                
  If any previous conclusion cannot be recovered reliably:                      
  mark it as [NOT RECOVERED]                                                    
  instead of inventing it.                                                      
                                                                                
  ============================================================                  
  1. FIRST ACTION — ORIENTATION                                                 
  ============================================================                  
                                                                                
  Before making any decision:                                                   
                                                                                
  1. Read CLAUDE.md.                                                            
  2. Inspect git status.                                                        
  3. Inspect the current repository tree.                                       
  4. Identify all available prior-phase artifacts.                              
  5. Locate the previous session transcript if necessary.                       
  6. Determine which decisions are already locked and which remain open.        
  7. Produce a short Resume Check.                                              
                                                                                
  The Resume Check must contain:                                                
                                                                                
  - Previous phases completed                                                   
  - Last known completed section                                                
  - Current phase                                                               
  - Available evidence                                                          
  - Missing evidence                                                            
  - Locked decisions                                                            
  - Unresolved decisions                                                        
  - External verification limitations                                           
  - Current git status                                                          
  - Whether any files have changed                                              
                                                                                
  DO NOT begin methodology redesign before this orientation is complete.

  2. Executive Summary

  Phase 2 produced a complete methodology. This phase converts it into a
  decision record, and in doing so found five things that change the picture —
  three of which move in the project's favour.

  1. The framing correction: nothing is locked, so implementation is blocked.
  Phase 2's "19 READY TO LOCK" was an evidence assessment, not an approval.
  Under a strict reading, zero decisions carry human approval, so the gate
  verdict in §25 is IMPLEMENTATION BLOCKED — and it would be blocked even if
  every evidence gap closed, because approval itself is the missing input. Two
  items (D1 network, E1 PRV placement) are additionally blocked on substance,
  not just paperwork.

  2. Phase 2 ran blind to Phase 1.5. Phase 2's own resume check states its
  predecessor reports were "neither in the repo nor in context". Consequence:
  Phase 2 never mentions Cyriac & Chacko 2026, the work Phase 1.5-B ranked as
  the #2 novelty threat. Phase 2's contribution reframing (AD3) was therefore
  written without the single most relevant 2026 paper. AD3 must not go to the 
  advisor in Phase 2's form.

  3. Cyriac & Chacko 2026 is a materially weaker threat than Phase 1.5-B feared.
  WebSearch was dead, so I triangulated: OpenAlex abstract + Crossref's 
  complete 23-item reference list. Findings (Direct Evidence for the abstract
  and the reference list; Inference for what they imply):
  - The agent "varies pumping station setpoints" — not PRVs. Across 23
    references there is no PRV-control reference; the single PRV mention is
    energy harvesting from PRVs (Gashteroodkhani 2020), not pressure control.
  - No leakage-model reference whatsoever — no Germanopoulos, Giustolisi,
    FAVAD/May/Lambert, or emitter literature. Their stated objective is energy 
    use and pipe-damage risk, not leakage volume.
  - Network = a proprietary calibrated Abu Dhabi WDN, not a public benchmark →
    low reproducibility, no comparability.
  - Tooling = EPANET + EPyT + Gym/Gymnasium + Stable-Baselines3. SAC's original 
    paper is cited; PPO's (Schulman 2017) is absent.
  - "Transferable" reads as an assertion about methodology portability, not a
    demonstrated cross-network experiment.

    → The "continuous action space for RL pressure control" framing is genuinely
    taken (Phase 1.5-B CH-5 confirmed — do not claim it). But leakage-driven 
    coordinated PRV+VSP under demand uncertainty on a public benchmark survives 
    with more room than Phase 1.5-B estimated.

  4. Phase 2's G1 closed, and it exposed a confound nobody had seen. I recovered
  Negm's Table 5-4 in full. PPO: 73.4 % water saved / 304 pressure violations /
  reward 780.0; DE: 65.9 % / 46 violations / 995.2. Negm's own words: PPO
  "exploited the leakage objective at the expense of the pressure violation 
  objective." But the table splits into two clean clusters — tuned agents and
  classical optimisers at ~65 %/46–78 violations, versus untuned on-policy
  agents at ~73 %/264–304 — and Negm states "All untuned DRL algorithms
  performed less favourably." So the reward-hacking outcome is confounded across
  three causes: binary violation term, no hyperparameter tuning, and a 
  20,000-step budget. Consequence: ablation A6 (binary vs β=1 vs β=2) is 
  promoted to Tier 1, because without it the project cannot attribute any
  improvement to its reward redesign.

  5. Two Phase-2 open items are now resolvable, and two more gaps closed. U7
  (CO₂) becomes decidable — Negm computes CO₂ from leakage via a fixed factor 
  176.7 kgCO₂/ML, so it is a linear transform of leakage carrying zero
  independent information (visible as perfect collinearity in Table 5-4). U5/G3
  closed — Hu placed PRVs on Net3 by "four valves with the greatest impact on 
  leakage … through a pre-experiment using a GA", which reveals a
  placement–objective co-optimisation bias and promotes E1 to research-critical.
  G6 closed — Vicente et al. 2016, cited repeatedly in Proposal and Seminar but
  absent from the corpus, is now fully identified (ASCE JWRPM 142(2):04015061).
  And a new close prior work surfaced: Pei, Hoang, Fu & Butler 2025, "Real-Time
  Pump Scheduling in WDNs Using Deep Reinforcement Learning," ASCE JWRPM 
  151(6):04025012 — unknown to every prior phase.

  Answers to your six closing questions, up front:

  1. Already decided: nothing. Zero approvals on record.
  2. You should decide: 8 researcher items (§5) — most urgently B4's threshold
     protocol, Q1 Python/EPANET versions, H2 β, J2 seeds.
  3. Ask your advisor: 7 items (§6) — D1 (network) and AD3-equivalent 
     (contribution reframing) first; then E1, plus four text corrections.
  4. Still needs evidence: 4 items (§7) — only G7 genuinely blocks a decision,
     and even that is bypassable by a declared sensitivity protocol.
  5. Methodology actually ready: 21 of 40 decisions have sufficient evidence and
     need only your signature; the specification in §23 is internally
     consistent with one real contradiction found (§24, C-2) and three tensions
     requiring declaration.
  6. Exactly what blocks implementation: D1 and E1 substantively (observation
     and action dimensionality derive from network topology, so not one line of
     the environment can be written), plus the absence of approval on everything
     else.

  3.2 Inherited conflicts between phases — resolved here

  #: X1
  Conflict: P1.5: "all three closest works used PDA" ↔ P1.5-B CH-1: "invalid,
  \bPDA\b=0" ↔ P2 §6.5 listing DDA as "compatible with Negm"
  Resolution: P1.5-B is correct. Independently re-verified: in Negm, \bPDA\b=0,
  \bDDA\b=0, demand-driven=0; "pressure dependent" appears only for  leakage
  (eq. 2-9). The PDA case therefore rests on construct  validity, native EPANET
   2.2 support, Abdy Sayyed 2015 and Mahdavi  1400 — not on field practice.
  Decision unchanged; its justification is corrected.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: X2
  Conflict: P2 §11.0 left the PPO-specific reward-hacking claim as Pending 
  Verification (G1) ↔ P1.5-B V-4/V-5 reported it verified
  Resolution: P1.5-B is correct, and I recovered the whole table. G1 → closed.
  Confidence High.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: X3
  Conflict: P1.5 "65.2 % leakage reduction" ↔ P2 "73.4 %"
  Resolution: Not a conflict. Negm's abstract reports the best tuned algorithm
  (TQC, 65.2 %); ch. 7 reports the best raw leakage (PPO, 73.4 %). Both
  correct, different selection criteria.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: X4
  Conflict: P1.5 CE-9 "~10 s, out of scope for real-time" ↔ Negm "0.3–0.4 s,
  qualifies for real-time"
  Resolution: Scale-dependent. DMA level < 0.4 s ✅; whole system (SZ08, 1,988
  nodes) ~10 s ❌. Net3 (92 nodes) sits in the first regime. Any  real-time 
  claim must state the scale.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: X5
  Conflict: P2 §12.1 "on-policy better in dynamic environments — verified" ↔
  P1.5-B CH-2 "policy-driven couldn't match their hybrid alternatives"
  Resolution: Both quotes are genuine, from different chapters. Ch. 5
  (background
  leakage, quasi-static): metaheuristics and tuned DRL win. Ch. 6 (bursts,
  dynamic): on-policy wins. Regime determines the result. P2 §12.1's
  justification table omitted this caveat and was overstated. Recorded in I4.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: X6
  Conflict: P1.5-B G-7 "does Net3 even have PRVs? unverified" ↔ P2 "standard
  Net3
  has no PRVs, must be added"
  Resolution: P2 correct, now with strong indirect confirmation: Hu had to add
  four valves. Direct .inp check still deferred to Step 0 (Confidence
  Medium-High).

  3.3 Re-verifications performed in this phase (Tier 1, primary full text)

  All from pre-extracted corpus text — no PDF re-extraction, no repetition of 
  the literature review.

  (a) Negm 2024 Table 5-4, "Key results – Jowitt & Xu" (p. 130–131) — recovered 
  complete:

  ┌────────────┬─────────┬─────────┬──────────────┬───────┬────────┬───────┐

  ├────────────┼─────────┼─────────┼──────────────┼───────┼────────┼───────┤
  │ TRPO       │ 800.5   │ 73.2    │ 264          │ 301.7 │ 259    │ 27    │
  ├────────────┼─────────┼─────────┼──────────────┼───────┼────────┼───────┤
  │ PPO        │ 780.0   │ 73.4    │ 304          │ 302.5 │ 216    │ 26.6  │
  ├────────────┼─────────┼─────────┼──────────────┼───────┼────────┼───────┤
  │ Recurrent  │ 798.4   │ 73.4    │ 300          │ 302.5 │ 401    │ 27    │
  │ PPO        │         │         │              │       │        │       │
  ├────────────┼─────────┼─────────┼──────────────┼───────┼────────┼───────┤
  │ DDPG       │ 525.0   │ 44.1    │ 48           │ 181.1 │ 558    │ 22.1  │
  ├────────────┼─────────┼─────────┼──────────────┼───────┼────────┼───────┤
  │ A2C        │ 822.5   │ 73.2    │ 300          │ 301.8 │ 321.5  │ 22.7  │
  └────────────┴─────────┴─────────┴──────────────┴───────┴────────┴───────┘

  Source: Negm 2024 PhD thesis, Lancaster University, Table 5-4. Evidence type: 
  Tier 1 (thesis, primary full text). Confidence: High. Direct evidence.

  (b) Reward-hacking quote, verbatim (Negm p. 130): "PPO and Recurrent PPO
  agents exploited the leakage objective at the expense of the pressure 
  violation objective both saving the highest amount of water at 73.4% and
  violating 304 and 300 pressure limits respectively." Tier 1. High. Direct 
  evidence.

  (c) The confound (new — no prior phase identified it). Direct evidence: Negm
  p. 131 — "The DRL algorithm's lower performance can be explained by their
  untuned parameters … All untuned DRL algorithms performed less favourably than
  the three benchmark optimisation algorithms." And p. 142: in SZ08 "DRL models
  were not hyperparameter tuned as the process would be too computationally
  expensive." Inference (Medium-High): the 73 %/300-violation cluster is exactly
  the untuned set and the 65 %/48-violation cluster is exactly the tuned +
  classical set, so reward-hacking magnitude is entangled with tuning and
  budget. This is inference, not Negm's claim.

  (d) Negm's demand model. Direct evidence: word-boundary counts over the full
  text — \bPDA\b = 0, \bDDA\b = 0, demand-driven = 0; all four "pressure
  dependent" occurrences refer to leakage. Inference (Medium-High): Negm ran
  EPANET's default DDA. Absence of the term is not proof; I do not present this 
  as direct evidence.

  (e) Hu's PRV placement on Net3 (closes U5/G3), verbatim: "the network was
  modified as follows: (1) Four valves with the greatest impact on leakage were 
  added to the network through a pre-experiment using a GA. (2) All simple
  control rules of pumps 10, 335, and pipe 330 were deleted. Pipe 330 was
  closed." Tier 1. High. Direct evidence.

  (f) Confirmations bundled: Net3 = "2 raw water sources, 2 pump stations, 3
  elevated storage tanks, 92 nodes, and 117 pipes", North Marin Water District,
  Kentucky Hydraulic Model Database, 24 h in 1 h intervals (Hu, Systems). Demand
  uncertainty = two-step temporal × spatial multipliers, truncated normal on
  (1−Δ, 1+Δ), large consumers excluded from randomisation, initial tank levels
  U(0,1); MARL range 0.7–1.3 (Δ=0.3) with "All the test cases are randomly 
  generated from the same distribution as the training cases" (both Hu papers → 
  both tested in-distribution only). Hu built the environment with WNTR. Hu eq. 
  (17): H_tank,fin ≥ H_tank,ini — "to exclude the solutions of water
  overconsumption in the tanks" → the tank-recovery constraint is a verified 
  hard constraint in prior work, not our invention. Pump efficiency "simplified 
  to 0.75". "water loss dominates the scheduling process, and the scheduling
  solutions … are mainly affected by the demand pattern of consumers rather than
  the energy tariff." CO₂ = leakage × "176.7 KgCO₂/million litres dictated by
  the UK's government values" → mathematically collinear with leakage.
  Real-time: "<0.4 s per prediction on a DMA level" vs "around 10 s per
  prediction" whole-system. Computation per scheduling case (Hu Table 3): GA
  1586–1699 s, PSO 1181–1291 s, DE 1408–1475 s; PPO training 5479–5833 s once,
  then application 0.42 s. All Tier 1, High, direct evidence.

  B3 — PDA vs DDA 🔴 research-critical

  Field: Decision
  Content: Demand model for training and for evaluation
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Current Status
  Content: Phase 2 marked READY TO LOCK; not approved
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Question
  Content: Is PDA mandatory in both phases, and should DDA survive only as a
  controlled ablation?
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Evidence

  H1 — Reward form 🔴 research-critical

  Field: Decision
  Content: Mathematical form, normalisation and term structure of the reward
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Current Status
  Content: Phase 2 recommended Option A (revised); not approved
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Question
  Content: Lock a four-term weighted reward with a continuous violation term and

  global normalisation?
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Evidence
  Content: Negm eq. 4-3: R = Σ[(v_after − v_before)·scale₁ + ((Q_lb − 
  Q_la)/Q_lb)·scale₂] with v ∈ {0,1} (below 10 m or above 70 m). Negm  Table 
  5-4 + p. 130: that reward produced PPO at 73.4 % leakage / 304  violations,
  "exploited the leakage objective at the expense of the pressure violation
  objective". Negm §4.2.4: 6:1 best leakage, 1:5 best violations, 3:1 best
  overall. Ledesma 2020 + 2024: the four canonical WDN control objectives are
  economic, safety, smoothness, water quality. Ma et al. 2024: frequent pump
  switching causes "wear of pump  units". Hu MARL (new): "water loss dominates 
  the scheduling process, and the scheduling solutions … are mainly affected by
   the demand  pattern … rather than the energy tariff". Hu MARL eq. (17):
  H_tank,fin  ≥ H_tank,ini as a hard constraint
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Source
  Content: Negm 2024 §4.2.4 & Table 5-4; Ledesma 2020/2024; Ma 2024; Hu MARL
  2023
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Evidence Type
  Content: Tier 1 ×4
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Confidence
  Content: High
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Options
  Content: (A) revised four-term weighted · (B) constrained / Lagrangian · (C)
  safety filter
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Scientific Advantages
  Content: (A) is literally approved innovation 2; works with stock PPO;
  comparable to Negm; interpretable weights · (B) constraint not tradeable ·
  (C) run-time guarantee
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Scientific Disadvantages
  Content: (A) demotes the constraint to a tradeable term — with enough leakage
  weight the agent may violate · (B) high implementation complexity, G9 open ·
  (C) needs a nominal model, in tension with the model-free spirit, not 
  realistic in 7 months
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Experimental Consequences
  Content: Change ⇒ retrain everything and redo the weight sweep
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Scope Consequences
  Content: None — this is exactly approved innovation 2
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Reproducibility Consequences
  Content: High — weights, β, ν^scale, L^ref, E^ref in config
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Implementation Consequences
  Content: Low–medium
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Risk
  Content: Medium — a poorly calibrated ν^scale with β=2 lets the violation term

  dominate and collapses the agent into an ultra-conservative
  high-pressure/high-leakage policy ⇒ test T9 mandatory
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: What Becomes Invalid If Changed Later?
  Content: Every policy, the Pareto front, every ablation, and the entire answer

  to sub-question 2
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Recommendation
  Content: Option A with three structural corrections to Negm's form: (i)
  ν_{j,t}
  continuous, in metres (removes indifference between 9.9 m and 2 m); (ii)
  global normalisation against a reference scenario (removes the per-node
  distortion that equally weights a trivial-leak node and a major-leak node);
  (iii) absolute rather than differential form (comparability with baselines).
  Energy term with fixed η = 0.75 (Hu) and no tariff modelling — Hu's evidence
  says tariff is not the driver, so omitting it keeps scope tight and is
  defensible. Tank-recovery  constraint mandatory — now an evidence-backed hard
   constraint (Hu eq. 17), not our invention. Option B → H3; Option C → future
  work
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Decision Owner
  Content: Researcher
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Approval Required
  Content: No (matches approved innovation 2)
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Can It Be Locked Now?
  Content: YES
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Reason
  Content: High-confidence evidence; each correction closes a documented failure

  mode; scope untouched

  equal tuning and equal budget is the only  experiment that isolates (a) —
  without it the project cannot claim its reward redesign did the work

   an argument favouring PPO over TD3/DDPG. Precedent: Hu E-PPO, Tsiami 2025,
  Negm. Negm Table 5-4: PPO had the fastest training time (216 s).
  Counter-evidence — Negm ch. 5: "policy driven methods (TRPO, PPO, Recurrent

  J1 — Training budget

  Field: Decision
  Content: Total timesteps per (algorithm × weight × seed)
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Current Status
  Content: Phase 2 recommended 1–2 M; not approved
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Question
  Content: What budget makes an algorithm comparison meaningful?
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Evidence
  Content: Negm, verbatim: "All DRL methods were subject to an identical 20,000 
  timesteps of training"; "all training loops were limited to 20,000 timesteps
  for fair comparison". Arithmetic: 20,000 ÷ 2048 ≈ 10 policy  updates; 20,000 
  ÷ 24 ≈ 833 episodes. Hu MARL: "The number of episodes in the MADDPG training
  procedure was set to 20,000" — i.e. 20,000 episodes ≈ 480,000 steps, 24× 
  Negm's budget. Hu E-PPO Table 3: PPO training 5479–5833 s. Hardware: Negm AMD
   Ryzen 4700U (CPU-only); Hu RTX 3070 + i7-11700K — so their wall-clock
  numbers are not mutually comparable
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Source
  Content: Negm 2024 §5; Hu 2023 ×2
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Evidence Type
  Content: Tier 1 ×3
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Confidence
  Content: High
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Options
  Content: (1) 1–2 M timesteps · (2) 200–500 k · (3) 20 k (corpus practice)
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Scientific Advantages
  Content: (1) ≥500 policy updates; conventional order for continuous control;
  Hu
  succeeded at ~480 k so 1 M gives margin
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Scientific Disadvantages
  Content: (3) 10 policy updates is effectively no training — any comparison at
  that budget measures sensitivity to initialisation rather than algorithm
  capability
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Experimental Consequences
  Content: Multiplies total compute directly
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Scope Consequences
  Content: None (completion, not change)
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Reproducibility Consequences
  Content: High
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Implementation Consequences
  Content: Low
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Risk
  Content: Low scientifically; Medium on schedule
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: What Becomes Invalid If Changed Later?
  Content: Every comparison. This is the most expensive avoidable error
  available
  — it is exactly the documented flaw in the reference work
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Recommendation
  Content: 1–2 M timesteps, n_steps 2048 (or a multiple of 24 with parallel
  envs), batch_size 64, n_epochs 10, γ = 0.99, lr = 3e-4, clip = 0.2 as the
  starting point (SB3 defaults, matching Negm's documented configuration) with
  a declared, equal tuning budget. Report the four  cost quantities separately 
  and never conflate them: (A) training wall-clock, (B) inference/decision time
   per step, (C) environment interactions, (D) hydraulic-solver evaluations per
   baseline
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Decision Owner
  Content: Researcher
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Approval Required
  Content: No
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Can It Be Locked Now?
  Content: YES
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Reason
  Content: Direct evidence that the prior budget was an order of magnitude short

  of field norms

  K1 — Demand-uncertainty mechanism

  Field: Decision

  ────────────────────────────────────────

  ────────────────────────────────────────

  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Evidence
  Content: Hu E-PPO, verbatim: "The randomisation of water demand … was carried
  out in two steps to mimic the time and space effects. Firstly, the general
  default demand pattern … multiplied by hourly random  multipliers … Secondly,
   the base demand … product of the nodal random  multiplier and the default
  base demand … Both random multipliers of time and space follow the truncated 
  normal distribution … restricted to … (1 − Δ, 1 + Δ)"; "The initial water 
  levels of the tanks follow a  uniform distribution of (0, 1)"; large
  consumers — "… compared to the general consumer and do not apply a 
  randomisation method to it". Hu  MARL: multipliers "follow a truncated normal
   distribution with a range from 0.7 to 1.3" (Δ=0.3); "All the test cases are
  randomly generated from the same distribution as the training cases".
  Proposal: only "فرصمم‌ک طیارش و فرصم جوا تاعاس ،یداع فرصم طیارش" — three

  K2 — Δ train/test separation

  Field: Decision

  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Current Status

  training cases". → both Hu papers tested in-distribution only. Hu E-PPO,
  verbatim: "when the environment has great uncertainty, it is difficult to 
  learn an optimal strategy network, as the state transition becomes blurred.
  The selection of the agent tends to be conservative"

  phase — the qualitative claim is verbatim-confirmed, the numbers are not
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Options
  Content: (1) Train {0.3, 0.6}; test in-distribution + held-out 0.9 · (2) train

  on all three · (3) train 0.3 only

  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: Scientific Disadvantages

  measurement at all
  ────────────────────────────────────────

  Δ=0.9,

   movement. In parallel, plot the  leakage↔violation-volume Pareto front for

   whole picture, and the front is the scientific answer to sub-question 2. The

  arbitrarily, and that derivation reported

  Decision · Status · Question: Statistical protocol · P2 recommended · What is
  realistic for a Master's thesis?
  Evidence · Source · Type · Confidence: Negm: no seeds, 3 episodes. Hu: 3
  repetitions with variance bands, 100 test cases. Inference: at 5 seeds
  significance tests have low power ⇒ effect size must lead · Tier 1 ×2 + Tier
  6 · High
  Options · Sci. Advantages / Disadvantages: (1) minimum defensible (2) ideal ·
  Adv: both are achievable; the distinction is honest · Dis: demanding the
  ideal risks an infeasible design
  Exp. / Scope / Repro. / Impl. Consequences · Risk · Invalidated-if-changed:
  Analysis
  only / None (completion) / High / Low · Low · Nothing (re-analysable)
  Recommendation · Owner · Approval · Lock? · Reason: MINIMUM DEFENSIBLE: unit
  of
  replication = training  seed (not episode — episodes within a seed are not
  independent); ≥5 seeds × ≥100 unseen test episodes; two-stage aggregation
  (within-seed mean, then between-seed statistics); mean ± SD + 95  % bootstrap
   CI; box plots per method; Mann–Whitney U (non-parametric, small n); Cliff's 
  delta effect size  leading the interpretation,  p-values supporting;
  Holm–Bonferroni across multiple method comparisons; all seeds  reported 
  including diverged  ones. IDEAL: 10 seeds, 200 test episodes, pre-registered
  analysis plan, paired tests on common scenario seeds. Honesty rule locked as 
  a  writing rule: where CIs overlap, state that the  difference is not 
  detectable — do not compare means and declare "better" · Researcher · No ·
  YES · Realistic, and it doubles the field's current standard

  8. Network Decision Analysis

  A. FACT / EVIDENCE. Jowitt & Xu (Negm Table 5-5, verbatim): 25 junctions, 37
  pipes, 3 valves, 0 pumps, 3 tanks/reservoirs, 1 DMA, "Medium" — independently
  confirmed by the Mosetlhe thesis. SZ08: 1,988 / 2,022 / 32 / 26 pumps / 11 /
  19 DMAs, "Extra Large", proprietary (Northumbrian Water). Net3 (Hu, Systems,
  verbatim): 2 raw water sources, 2 pump stations, 3 elevated storage tanks, 92 
  nodes, 117 pipes; North Marin Water District, Novato CA; available from the
  Kentucky Hydraulic Model Database (Ormsbee et al. 2022); 24 h horizon in 1 h
  intervals. Hu (MARL): Net3 is "one of the most popular benchmarks … data
  availability and flexibility for modification", and was used for joint pump 
  and valve control after adding four valves. Anytown and D-Town: used with DRL
  for pumps by Hajgató, Paál & Gyires-Tóth 2020. Proposal: the network is not 
  fixed — "عجرم درادناتسا یاهه‌کبش زا یکی" is permitted. Negm: 73.4 % leakage
  reduction on J&X versus a maximum of 0.693 % on SZ08.

  B. INFERENCE. (i) Choosing J&X makes the approved objective 1 and innovation 1
  unexecutable — coordinated PRV and pump control is impossible on a network
  with zero pumps. (ii) Leakage-reduction percentage is not a transferable 
  quantity across networks; a single large number on a small benchmark says
  almost nothing about real applicability. (iii) No standard benchmark carries
  both PRVs and VSPs natively (G10 closed negatively), so adding PRVs is 
  unavoidable on any pump-bearing candidate — which makes E1 an unavoidable
  methodological exposure rather than a detail.

  are fixed commitments.
