  23. Draft Methodology Specification

  ▎ Tagging rule strictly applied. Nothing carries [LOCKED], because no 
  ▎ researcher decision and no advisor approval is on record. Everything is 
  ▎ [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] or [UNRESOLVED].

  1. Problem formulation — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] A POMDP solved as an
  MDP over approximated states (the agent observes neither future demand nor all
  nodes). Horizon 24 h; decision interval 1 h (consistent with Belfadil, Negm
  and Hu); episode = 24 steps; terminal at horizon end. Dynamics: each step
  EPANET performs a steady-state solve at the hour's demand with applied
  settings; tank level carries forward by volume balance — Negm's "EPS redefined
  as a chain of steady-state solves". [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Declared 
  limitation: no transient/water-hammer dynamics, so cyclic-fatigue reduction is
  not directly provable; only proxies are measured. [UNRESOLVED — A4, advisor]

  2. Hydraulic simulation — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] EPANET 2.2 (native
  Wagner PDA); WNTR ≥1.0 for network construction and scenario generation;
  EpanetSimulator for execution (mandatory — WNTRSimulator ignores emitter
  coefficients). Declared limitation: PDA parameters are necessarily global
  under EpanetSimulator. Demand model PDA both in training and evaluation; DDA
  retained as controlled ablation A7. P₀ = 0, 1/e = 0.5. Mandatory pre-training
  gate V1–V5. [UNRESOLVED — B4] P_f, p^min_service, p^max; protocol
  recommendation p^min = 20 m reference with mandatory sensitivity S3 over {10,
  14, 20, 25} and P_f set slightly above p^min. [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED]
  EPANET 2.3 / native FAVAD → future work.

  3. Network — [UNRESOLVED — D1, advisor] Recommendation: EPANET Net3 (92 nodes,
  117 pipes, 2 raw sources, 2 pump stations, 3 elevated tanks; North Marin
  Water District; Kentucky Hydraulic Model Database). [UNRESOLVED — E1, advisor]
  PRV placement; recommendation: two sets — P-GA (four PRVs via GA
  pre-experiment on leakage impact, replicating Hu, carrying the main results)
  and P-OP (four PRVs at operationally motivated locations chosen without
  reference to the leakage objective, carrying a full primary-metrics
  reproduction as sensitivity S9). [UNRESOLVED — D2] Secondary network;
  recommendation Jowitt & Xu for a PRV-only sub-experiment, execute-decision in
  month 6. [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] The final .inp, recording every
  modification, version-controlled in git.

  4. Leakage model — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Q^leak_j = k_j · p_j^{1.18},
  k_j ∝ Σ_i L_ij, k_j < 0.196 Ls⁻¹m⁻⁰·⁵ (background regime). Mandatory 
  calibration: scale k_j so total leakage in the reference scenario equals 10 % 
  and 20 % of total demand (two experimental levels). WNTR-native leakage
  rejected (α locked at 0.5). Pipe-level model retained as a sensitivity.
  Mandatory sensitivity S1 over N₁ ∈ {0.5, 1.0, 1.18, 1.2, 1.4}, hard ceiling
  1.5.

  5. Demand model — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] PDA (Wagner): d_{j,t} = 0 for
  p ≤ P₀; D_{j,t}((p−P₀)/(P_f−P₀))^{1/e} for P₀ < p < P_f; D_{j,t} for p ≥ P_f.
  DSR = Σ_{t,j} d_{j,t} / Σ_{t,j} D_{j,t}. Leakage flows via the emitter path, 
  consumer demand via the PDA function — two separate paths, which is what makes
  leakage and service shortfall separable. [UNRESOLVED — G2] exact numerical
  PDA↔emitter interaction, resolved by test V4.

  6. State — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] o_t = [p^M_t, q^F_t, h^tank_t, 
  a_{t-1}, sin(2πt/24), cos(2πt/24)]. Selection rule for M: global critical node
  + immediate downstream node of each PRV + one representative per pressure
  zone. Two hard prohibitions: nodal Q^leak never in the main observation (a
  model quantity, not a measurement ⇒ non-deployable policy); the true demand 
  multiplier never in the observation (future-information leakage ⇒ sub-question
  1 vacuous). Permitted: the effect of demand. Normalisation statistics from
  training only, stored and published. S3 restricted to one oracle run; S1 in
  ablation A8. [UNRESOLVED — F2] numeric sensor list, fixed after Step 0.

  7. Action — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] s^PRV_{v,t} = clip(s̃_{v,t}, 
  s^PRV_{v,t-1} ± δ_v), ω_{u,t} = clip(ω̃_{u,t}, ω_{u,t-1} ± δ_u); s^PRV_v ∈ 
  [p^min_service, s̄_v] (target outlet head, metres), ω_u ∈ [0.7, 1.0]
  (mechanical constraint, Hu); s̄_v from 1,000 random rollouts (Hu's empirical
  method). Policy outputs tanh rescaled to physical range ⇒ invalid actions
  structurally impossible. ARR logged: realised setting read back from EPANET
  each step; reported per actuator and aggregated. Pure incremental in ablation
  A11. [UNRESOLVED — G2 sensitivity] δ at 10 % reference, S8 optional.

  8. Reward — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] r_t = −[w_L L̃_t + w_V Ṽ_t + w_S S̃_t 
  + w_E Ẽ_t], all terms dimensionless and globally normalised against a
  reference scenario; Ṽ_t = (1/|J|)Σ_j(ν_{j,t}/ν^scale)^β with ν_{j,t} = max(0, 
  p^min − p_{j,t}) + max(0, p_{j,t} − p^max) in metres; Ẽ_t with η = 0.75, no 
  tariff modelling; hard constraint H_tank,fin ≥ H_tank,ini. Mandatory
  pre-training battery T1, T2, T3, T4, T6, T7, T9, T10 — T3 and T7 are hard 
  gates. [UNRESOLVED — H2] β: reference 2, determined by mandatory ablation A6
  over {binary, 1, 2}. [UNRESOLVED — H5] final weights explicitly deferred:
  sweep w_V/w_L ∈ {1/7, 1/5, 1/3, 1, 3, 5, 7} at identical budget and seeds,
  report the full Pareto front, then select by feasibility-first. [UNRESOLVED — 
  H3] constrained formulation, Tier 3, execute-decision in month 5.
  [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] safety filter → future work.

  9. PPO — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] PPO retained unchanged as the approved
  primary algorithm. Starting configuration n_steps = 2048, batch_size = 64,
  n_epochs = 10, γ = 0.99, lr = 3e-4, clip = 0.2 (SB3 defaults, matching Negm's
  documented setup), with a declared equal tuning budget. Justification carries 
  a regime caveat: the on-policy advantage has evidence only in the dynamic
  regime; in the quasi-static regime the opposite holds. [UNRESOLVED — I4, 
  advisor] removal of the "on-policy ⇒ smaller sim-to-real gap" non-sequitur.
  MADDPG / multi-agent excluded from the primary methodology.

  10. Comparator — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] SAC as the single learning
  comparator at equal timestep budget, equal seed count and equal tuning trials.
  [UNRESOLVED — I3] Recurrent PPO as an architectural ablation, Tier 3.

  11. Training — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] 1–2 M timesteps per (algorithm ×
  weight × seed) ⇒ ≥500 policy updates. Fixed budget, no test-based early 
  stopping; separate validation set for checkpoint and weight selection; both
  final and best checkpoints logged; learning curves reported as mean ± SD
  across seeds. The four cost quantities reported separately and never 
  conflated: (A) training wall-clock, (B) inference/decision time, (C)
  environment interactions, (D) hydraulic-solver evaluations. [UNRESOLVED — J2]
  seeds: 5 recommended with conditional escalation to 10. [UNRESOLVED — J3]
  sweep cost reduction: reduced-budget sweep then full budget for the chosen
  weight plus one adjacent ratio, reduction declared.

  12. Demand uncertainty — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Two-step
  truncated-normal multipliers (hourly × nodal) on (1−Δ, 1+Δ); large Net3
  consumers excluded from randomisation; initial tank level U(0,1). Train Δ ∈ 
  {0.3, 0.6}; Δ = 0.9 fully held out for OOD testing; out-of-phase demand
  pattern as a second OOD test; ≥100 test episodes per seed. Step-2 feasibility
  check on Δ = 0.9. Historical/SCADA profiles and full Monte Carlo excluded.
  Scalar perturbation optional as a cheap stress test.

  13. Evaluation — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Five primary metrics (leakage
  volume absolute and relative; violation volume in metre·hours; violation
  count; maximum violation; DSR); eight secondary (mean pressure, pressure
  variability, energy kWh, actuator movement, tank recovery, ARR, inference
  time, training time); learning diagnostics as listed in §17. CO₂ excluded
  (collinear with leakage). Hour-resolved reporting mandatory. [RECOMMENDATION —
  NOT LOCKED, PRE-REGISTRATION REQUIRED] feasibility-first lexicographic
  ranking with the safety threshold derived from the best baseline in Step 2,
  plus the leakage↔violation Pareto front reported in parallel.

  14. Baselines — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Mandatory: fixed-outlet PRV
  (tuned by explicit industrial criterion), TMPC, DE (documented hydraulic
  budget raised to plateau), single-objective PPO, SAC. Recommended:
  P-controller on the critical node, GA. Optional: PSO, Nelder-Mead. Rejected:
  MPC, MADDPG. Uniform fairness protocol: same network, scenarios, seeds,
  simulator, leakage model, constraints and locked test set; break-even ≈ 4
  operational cases reported.

  15. Ablation — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Tier 1: A14, A5, A12, A7, A6.
  Tier 2: A8, A9, A13, A11, A1–A4 (A2's number reported explicitly as the
  reward-hacking upper bound). Tier 3: A10, constrained arm. Fairness rule:
  identical budget, seeds and test set across all tiers.

  16. Sensitivity — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Mandatory: S1 (N₁), S2
  (leakage level), S3 (p^min), S6 (weight ratio), S9 (PRV placement). Covered
  elsewhere: S5 (by V1–V3), S7 (by A12), S10 (by the tuning budget). Optional:
  S4, S8. Method: one-factor-at-a-time at 3 seeds (declared reduction), reported
  as a tornado plot.

  17. Statistics — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Minimum defensible: unit of
  replication = training seed; ≥5 seeds × ≥100 unseen test episodes; two-stage
  aggregation; mean ± SD + 95 % bootstrap CI; box plots per method; Mann–Whitney
  U; Cliff's delta effect size leading the interpretation, p-values supporting;
  Holm–Bonferroni for multiple method comparisons; all seeds reported including
  diverged ones. Ideal: 10 seeds, 200 test episodes, pre-registered analysis
  plan, paired tests on common scenario seeds. Honesty rule: where CIs overlap,
  state that the difference is not detectable.

  18. Reproducibility — [RECOMMENDATION — NOT LOCKED] Full traceability chain
  and run-directory contents per §19; experiment ID scheme; three separate seed
  streams with the test stream never used in training; exact version pins plus
  pip freeze; hardware recorded; historical results never overwritten; modified
  .inp version-controlled. [UNRESOLVED — Q1] Python version; recommendation 3.12
  in a dedicated venv.

  ---

  24. Methodology Consistency Audit

  #: C-1
  Potential contradiction: PDA declared but DDA-only assumptions retained
  Verdict: ✅ Clean. PDA is specified for both phases; DDA appears only as
  ablation A7 with its own simulator configuration. Tests V1–V3 exist precisely
   to catch a silent fallback to DDA via the 0.07 m default
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-2
  Potential contradiction: Leakage metric without a leakage model
  Verdict: 🔴 REAL CONTRADICTION — but in the inherited documents, not in this 
  specification. The Proposal makes leakage reduction its primary objective
  while specifying no leakage model (emitter, FAVAD, N₁, leakage exponent all
  zero occurrences). §23 item 4 resolves it. This  is why the Proposal cannot 
  be implemented as written, and it is a  completion rather than a scope 
  change. Flagged in §22
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-3
  Potential contradiction: Future demand inside the state
  Verdict: ✅ Clean, and structurally prohibited. F4 bans the true demand
  multiplier explicitly; only its effect (observed pressures and flows) is
  admitted
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-4
  Potential contradiction: Violation threshold inconsistent with PDA
  Verdict: 🟡 TENSION — identified and mitigated, must be declared. If P_f
  equals
  p^min, DSR and violation count become structurally correlated and are not
  independent metrics. Mitigation: set P_f slightly above p^min; declare the
  residual correlation in the interpretation. Not resolved  by construction — 
  resolved by declaration
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-5
  Potential contradiction: Reward objective inconsistent with evaluation metric
  Verdict: ✅ Clean, and deliberately aligned. Reward terms L̃, Ṽ, S̃, Ẽ map onto
  primary metrics leakage volume and violation volume and secondary metrics
  actuator movement and energy. One deliberate asymmetry: DSR and maximum
  violation are evaluated but not rewarded — intentional, so that reward
  hacking is detectable by a metric the agent is not optimising. This is a
  feature; it is recorded so it is not mistaken for an inconsistency
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-6
  Potential contradiction: PRV action range inconsistent with physics
  Verdict: ✅ Clean. Upper bound derived empirically from 1,000 rollouts rather
  than assumed; VSP bounded at [0.7, 1.0] on mechanical grounds; rate-limit
  projection guarantees inter-step feasibility; tanh rescaling makes
  out-of-range actions impossible. Residual physical gap  acknowledged: the
  three-state PRV means a valid command may still be unrealised — which is
  exactly what ARR measures rather than assumes away
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-7
  Potential contradiction: Baseline using a different simulator
  Verdict: ✅ Clean. M3 requires identical simulator, network, leakage model,
  PDA
  parameters, constraints, scenarios and locked test set for every baseline
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-8
  Potential contradiction: Demand uncertainty in training but not testing
  Verdict: ✅ Clean, and stronger than the prior work. Uncertainty is present in

  train, validation and test, with Δ = 0.9 held out — an advance on both Hu
  papers, which tested in-distribution only
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-9
  Potential contradiction: Generalisation claimed without cross-network testing
  Verdict: ✅ Clean by construction. L3 is explicitly excluded and recorded as
  future work; L4 restricts the claim to "unseen demand scenarios and operating
   conditions, on the same network", with topology listed in the limitations
  chapter
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-10
  Potential contradiction: "Real-time" claimed without decision-time measurement
  Verdict: ✅ Clean, with a scale caveat. Inference time is a secondary metric;
  M3 mandates the four separate cost figures. Additional requirement: every
  real-time claim must state the scale — Negm's own evidence shows <0.4 s at
  DMA level versus ~10 s whole-system, and Net3 at 92 nodes sits in the first
  regime
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-11
  Potential contradiction: Energy metric without a pump model
  Verdict: ✅ Clean. The energy term uses η = 0.75 (Hu's documented
  simplification) with EPANET's pump curves supplying head and flow. Declared 
  simplification: constant rather than curve-derived efficiency; tariff
  modelling deliberately omitted on Hu's evidence that solutions track demand
  pattern, not tariff
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-12
  Potential contradiction: Smoothness metric without previous action
  Verdict: ✅ Clean. a_{t-1} is in the S2 observation, which is what makes S̃_t
  computable — a dependency the state design was specified to satisfy
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-13 (added)
  Potential contradiction: Attribution confound: reward redesign vs tuning vs
  budget
  Verdict: 🟡 TENSION, mitigated by design. Negm's reward-hacking outcome is
  entangled across three causes. Mitigation: A6 at equal tuning and budget
  isolates the reward's share. Unresolved unless A6 actually runs — which is
  why it was promoted to Tier 1
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  #: C-14 (added)
  Potential contradiction: Placement co-optimised with the measured objective
  Verdict: 🟡 TENSION, mitigated by design. P-GA carries the same optimistic
  bias
  Negm diagnosed. Mitigation: dual placement sets plus mandatory sensitivity S9
   plus an explicit statement that leakage-reduction percentage is
  siting-conditional

  Audit result: one real contradiction (C-2, inherited from the Proposal and 
  resolved by this specification) and three tensions (C-4, C-13, C-14) that are 
  managed by design and declaration rather than eliminated. No internal
  contradiction was found within the specification of §23 itself.

  ---

  IMPLEMENTATION BLOCKED

  Two distinct kinds of blockage, and they should not be conflated:

  (a) Substantive blockage — 2 items. D1 and E1. Even with full approval on
  everything else, not one line of the environment could be written: the
  observation dimension derives from the node count and sensor set, and the
  action dimension derives from the number of PRVs and pumps. Both come from the
  network and its valve placement. These are the real blockers.

  (b) Procedural blockage — 15 items. Evidence is sufficient and recommendations
  are unambiguous, but no approval exists. This clears the moment you sign off;
  it requires no new evidence and no further analysis.

  One item (B4) is neither — it has a genuine evidence gap (G7) but the
  sensitivity protocol lets work proceed without waiting.

  No code was written. No environment was created. No EPANET run, no PPO or SAC 
  training, no experiment, no result file, no package installed.

  knowing about Cyriac. With your permission: write Phase 1.5 / 1.5-B / 2 / 3

  research-review/SKILL.md (635 lines in the working tree, 0 bytes in HEAD, at
  risk since Phase 1); and pre-register the feasibility-first ranking rule and
  evaluation protocol in docs/experiments.md. This is the structural fix for the
  failure mode that produced the incomplete AD3 — not "more care next time".

  2. This week — one advisor meeting, three items in this order.
  - D1 — the hard network conflict: the reference work's network has zero pumps;
    the approved objective 1 is coordinated PRV and pump control; these are
    incompatible.
  - A3 + the Proposal §1 contradiction — contribution position against Negm

  - A5 — the reference list.

  made explicit), A4, A1, I4, A6. Rationale for the ordering: A5 and the §1

  and action dimensions are unknown, so every line would be rewritten.

   هک تسا **WDN + DRL سدنهم و ،PhD/Master ریگت‌خس رواد ،دشرا رگشهوژپ** کی وت شقن 
  .دنک یسررب **adversarial** تروصه‌ب ار همانن‌ایاپ یلعف یاهب‌اختنا دیاب            
                                                                                
   ،دناه‌دش داهنشیپ Phase 1/2/3 رد هکنیا لیلد هب طقف ار یلبق یاهم‌یمصت زا مادکچ‌یه 
  .نکن ضرف تسرد                                                                 
                                                                                
   تروصه‌ب Net3 زا و نکن عافد یلبق یریگه‌جیتن زا **هکبش باختنا** یه‌رابرد صوصخه‌ب   
  .نکن تیامح ضرفش‌یپ                                                             
                                                                                
  :دوش صخشم رگید راب کی ،شیامزآ یارجا و دک نتشون زا لبق هک تسا نیا زاف نیا فده  
                                                                                
  ؟تسا مادک اًعقاو همانن‌ایاپ یارب هکبش نیرتهب .1                                 
   لثم دیدج یازجا و درک هدافتسا هیاپ ناونعه‌ب Negm یه‌کبش نامه زا ناوتی‌م ایآ .2   
  ؟درک هفاضا PRV و پمپ                                                          
  ؟دنکی‌م بارخ ار benchmark ای تسا عافدل‌باق و یملع یرییغت نینچ ایآ .3            
  ؟تسا رتهب یرگید یمومع یه‌کبش ای تسا باختنا نیرتهب اًعقاو Net3 ایآ .4            
  ؟دناع‌افدل‌باق و زاجم یتاحالصا هچ اًقیقد ،مینک حالصا ار یاه‌کبش دشاب رارق رگا .5  
   RQ، novelty، fairness، reproducibility، computation رب یرثا هچ باختنا نیا .6 
  ؟دراد شیامزآ یحارط و                                                          
   دراو نآ ساسا رب مناوتب هک هدب **ارجا لباق و نشور میمصت کی** ،تیاهن رد .7     
  implementation موش.                                                           
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  # CONTEXT هژورپ                                                               
                                                                                
  Deep زا هدافتسا اب Water Distribution Network رد راشف لرتنک یه‌رابرد همانن‌ایاپ 
  Reinforcement Learning تسا.                                                   
                                                                                
  :شور یه‌تسه                                                                    
                                                                                
  * EPANET                                                                      
  * WNTR                                                                        
  * PPO                                                                         
  * continuous action space                                                     
  * PRV control                                                                 
  * Variable-Speed Pump (VSP)                                                   
  * leakage minimization                                                        
  * pressure/service constraints                                                
  * stochastic demand                                                           
  * uncertainty/generalization                                                  
  * multi-objective reward                                                      
  RL و کیسالک یاهbaseline اب هسیاقم *                                           
                                                                                
  Research Question تسا هدیا نیا لوح اًبیرقت یلصا:                               
                                                                                
  ،نئمطمان و ریغتم یاضاقت طیارش رد ،پمپ و PRV گنهامه لرتنک اب دناوتی‌م PPO ایآ > 
   pressure/سیورس تیفیک هکنیا نودب ،دهد شهاک ار leakage و هدرک میظنت ار راشف    
  constraints ؟دنوش ضقن لوبقل‌باقریغ لکش هب                                      
                                                                                
  Proposal دنچ innovation زا یرایسب هک هدش صخشم تایبدا رورم زا دعب اما ،دراد    
  .دناه‌دوب دح زا شیب novelty هیلوا یاهاعدا                                      
                                                                                
  :دوش فیرعت ریز یاهاعدا اب دیابن novelty نیاربانب                              
                                                                                
  * continuous action راب نیلوا یارب                                            
  * EPANET + PPO راب نیلوا یارب                                                 
  * PRV + pump coordination راب نیلوا یارب                                      
  * multi-objective reward راب نیلوا یارب                                       
                                                                                
  .تسا هدش ادیپ اهاعدا نیا هیلع یدهاوش نوچ                                      
                                                                                
  ،attribution، observability ،شیامزآ یحارط** رد رتشیب یلامتحا یروآون ،نآ یاجه‌ب 
  PDA/DDA، uncertainty separation، Pareto evaluation و robustness analysis**    
  .دراد رارق

❯ همانن‌ایاپ یروآون و شیامزآ یحارط ،هکبش باختنا قیمع و لقتسم ینیبزاب :دیدج زاف # 
                                                                                
  وت شقن ##                                                                     
                                                                                
  .یتسین »یلبق یاهم‌یمصت یه‌دننکدییأت« ای »یسیوندک رایتسد« اًفرص زاف نیا رد وت     
                                                                                
   هک تسا **WDN + DRL سدنهم و ،PhD/Master ریگت‌خس رواد ،دشرا رگشهوژپ** کی وت شقن 
  .دنک یسررب **adversarial** تروصه‌ب ار همانن‌ایاپ یلعف یاهب‌اختنا دیاب            
                                                                                
   ،دناه‌دش داهنشیپ Phase 1/2/3 رد هکنیا لیلد هب طقف ار یلبق یاهم‌یمصت زا مادکچ‌یه 
  .نکن ضرف تسرد                                                                 
                                                                                
   تروصه‌ب Net3 زا و نکن عافد یلبق یریگه‌جیتن زا **هکبش باختنا** یه‌رابرد صوصخه‌ب   
  .نکن تیامح ضرفش‌یپ                                                             
                                                                                
  :دوش صخشم رگید راب کی ،شیامزآ یارجا و دک نتشون زا لبق هک تسا نیا زاف نیا فده  
                                                                                
  ؟تسا مادک اًعقاو همانن‌ایاپ یارب هکبش نیرتهب .1                                 
   لثم دیدج یازجا و درک هدافتسا هیاپ ناونعه‌ب Negm یه‌کبش نامه زا ناوتی‌م ایآ .2   
  ؟درک هفاضا PRV و پمپ                                                          
  ؟دنکی‌م بارخ ار benchmark ای تسا عافدل‌باق و یملع یرییغت نینچ ایآ .3            
  ؟تسا رتهب یرگید یمومع یه‌کبش ای تسا باختنا نیرتهب اًعقاو Net3 ایآ .4            
  ؟دناع‌افدل‌باق و زاجم یتاحالصا هچ اًقیقد ،مینک حالصا ار یاه‌کبش دشاب رارق رگا .5  
   RQ، novelty، fairness، reproducibility، computation رب یرثا هچ باختنا نیا .6 
  ؟دراد شیامزآ یحارط و                                                          
   دراو نآ ساسا رب مناوتب هک هدب **ارجا لباق و نشور میمصت کی** ،تیاهن رد .7     
  implementation موش.

  # CONTEXT هژورپ                                                               
                                                                                
  Deep زا هدافتسا اب Water Distribution Network رد راشف لرتنک یه‌رابرد همانن‌ایاپ 
  Reinforcement Learning تسا.                                                   
                                                                                
  :شور یه‌تسه                                                                    
                                                                                
  * EPANET                                                                      
  * WNTR                                                                        
  * PPO                                                                         
  * continuous action space                                                     
  * PRV control                                                                 
  * Variable-Speed Pump (VSP)                                                   
  * leakage minimization                                                        
  * pressure/service constraints                                                
  * stochastic demand                                                           
  * uncertainty/generalization                                                  
  * multi-objective reward                                                      
  RL و کیسالک یاهbaseline اب هسیاقم *                                           
                                                                                
  Research Question تسا هدیا نیا لوح اًبیرقت یلصا:                               
                                                                                
  ،نئمطمان و ریغتم یاضاقت طیارش رد ،پمپ و PRV گنهامه لرتنک اب دناوتی‌م PPO ایآ > 
   pressure/سیورس تیفیک هکنیا نودب ،دهد شهاک ار leakage و هدرک میظنت ار راشف    
  constraints ؟دنوش ضقن لوبقل‌باقریغ لکش هب                                      
                                                                                
  Proposal دنچ innovation زا یرایسب هک هدش صخشم تایبدا رورم زا دعب اما ،دراد    
  .دناه‌دوب دح زا شیب novelty هیلوا یاهاعدا                                      
                                                                                
  :دوش فیرعت ریز یاهاعدا اب دیابن novelty نیاربانب                              
                                                                                
  * continuous action راب نیلوا یارب                                            
  * EPANET + PPO راب نیلوا یارب                                                 
  * PRV + pump coordination راب نیلوا یارب                                      
  * multi-objective reward راب نیلوا یارب                                       
                                                                                
  .تسا هدش ادیپ اهاعدا نیا هیلع یدهاوش نوچ                                      
                                                                                
  ،attribution، observability ،شیامزآ یحارط** رد رتشیب یلامتحا یروآون ،نآ یاجه‌ب 
  PDA/DDA، uncertainty separation، Pareto evaluation و robustness analysis**    
  .دراد رارق

  # CONTEXT هژورپ                                                               
                                                                                
  Deep زا هدافتسا اب Water Distribution Network رد راشف لرتنک یه‌رابرد همانن‌ایاپ 
  Reinforcement Learning تسا.                                                   
                                                                                
  :شور یه‌تسه                                                                    
                                                                                
  * EPANET                                                                      
  * WNTR                                                                        
  * PPO                                                                         
  * continuous action space                                                     
  * PRV control                                                                 
  * Variable-Speed Pump (VSP)                                                   
  * leakage minimization                                                        
  * pressure/service constraints                                                
  * stochastic demand                                                           
  * uncertainty/generalization                                                  
  * multi-objective reward                                                      
  RL و کیسالک یاهbaseline اب هسیاقم *                                           
                                                                                
  Research Question تسا هدیا نیا لوح اًبیرقت یلصا:                               
                                                                                
  ،نئمطمان و ریغتم یاضاقت طیارش رد ،پمپ و PRV گنهامه لرتنک اب دناوتی‌م PPO ایآ > 
   pressure/سیورس تیفیک هکنیا نودب ،دهد شهاک ار leakage و هدرک میظنت ار راشف    
  constraints ؟دنوش ضقن لوبقل‌باقریغ لکش هب                                      
                                                                                
  Proposal دنچ innovation زا یرایسب هک هدش صخشم تایبدا رورم زا دعب اما ،دراد    
  .دناه‌دوب دح زا شیب novelty هیلوا یاهاعدا                                      
                                                                                
  :دوش فیرعت ریز یاهاعدا اب دیابن novelty نیاربانب                              
                                                                                
  * continuous action راب نیلوا یارب                                            
  * EPANET + PPO راب نیلوا یارب                                                 
  * PRV + pump coordination راب نیلوا یارب                                      
  * multi-objective reward راب نیلوا یارب                                       
                                                                                
  .تسا هدش ادیپ اهاعدا نیا هیلع یدهاوش نوچ                                      
                                                                                
  ،attribution، observability ،شیامزآ یحارط** رد رتشیب یلامتحا یروآون ،نآ یاجه‌ب 
  PDA/DDA، uncertainty separation، Pareto evaluation و robustness analysis**

  * EPANET                                                                      
  * WNTR                                                                        
  * PPO

  * pressure/service constraints                                                
  * stochastic demand                                                           
  * uncertainty/generalization

  RL و کیسالک یاهbaseline اب هسیاقم *

  ،نئمطمان و ریغتم یاضاقت طیارش رد ،پمپ و PRV گنهامه لرتنک اب دناوتی‌م PPO ایآ > 
   pressure/سیورس تیفیک هکنیا نودب ،دهد شهاک ار leakage و هدرک میظنت ار راشف    
  constraints ؟دنوش ضقن لوبقل‌باقریغ لکش هب                                      
                                                                                
  Proposal دنچ innovation زا یرایسب هک هدش صخشم تایبدا رورم زا دعب اما ،دراد

  * EPANET + PPO راب نیلوا یارب                                                 
  * PRV + pump coordination راب نیلوا یارب                                      
  * multi-objective reward راب نیلوا یارب                                       
                                                                                
  .تسا هدش ادیپ اهاعدا نیا هیلع یدهاوش نوچ                                      
                                                                                
  ،attribution، observability ،شیامزآ یحارط** رد رتشیب یلامتحا یروآون ،نآ یاجه‌ب 
  PDA/DDA، uncertainty separation، Pareto evaluation و robustness analysis**

  # CONTEXT هژورپ                                                               
                                                                                
  Deep زا هدافتسا اب Water Distribution Network رد راشف لرتنک یه‌رابرد همانن‌ایاپ 
  Reinforcement Learning تسا.                                                   
                                                                                
  :شور یه‌تسه                                                                    
                                                                                
  * EPANET                                                                      
  * WNTR                                                                        
  * PPO                                                                         
  * continuous action space                                                     
  * PRV control                                                                 
  * Variable-Speed Pump (VSP)                                                   
  * leakage minimization                                                        
  * pressure/service constraints                                                
  * stochastic demand                                                           
  * uncertainty/generalization                                                  
  * multi-objective reward                                                      
  RL و کیسالک یاهbaseline اب هسیاقم *                                           
                                                                                
  Research Question تسا هدیا نیا لوح اًبیرقت یلصا:                               
                                                                                
  ،نئمطمان و ریغتم یاضاقت طیارش رد ،پمپ و PRV گنهامه لرتنک اب دناوتی‌م PPO ایآ > 
   pressure/سیورس تیفیک هکنیا نودب ،دهد شهاک ار leakage و هدرک میظنت ار راشف    
  constraints ؟دنوش ضقن لوبقل‌باقریغ لکش هب                                      
                                                                                
  Proposal دنچ innovation زا یرایسب هک هدش صخشم تایبدا رورم زا دعب اما ،دراد    
  .دناه‌دوب دح زا شیب novelty هیلوا یاهاعدا                                      
                                                                                
  :دوش فیرعت ریز یاهاعدا اب دیابن novelty نیاربانب                              
                                                                                
  * continuous action راب نیلوا یارب                                            
  * EPANET + PPO راب نیلوا یارب                                                 
  * PRV + pump coordination راب نیلوا یارب                                      
  * multi-objective reward راب نیلوا یارب                                       
                                                                                
  .تسا هدش ادیپ اهاعدا نیا هیلع یدهاوش نوچ                                      
                                                                                
  ،attribution، observability ،شیامزآ یحارط** رد رتشیب یلامتحا یروآون ،نآ یاجه‌ب 
  PDA/DDA، uncertainty separation، Pareto evaluation و robustness analysis**    
  .دراد رارق

  وت شقن ##                                                                     
                                                                                
  .یتسین »یلبق یاهم‌یمصت یه‌دننکدییأت« ای »یسیوندک رایتسد« اًفرص زاف نیا رد وت     
                                                                                
   هک تسا **WDN + DRL سدنهم و ،PhD/Master ریگت‌خس رواد ،دشرا رگشهوژپ** کی وت شقن 
  .دنک یسررب **adversarial** تروصه‌ب ار همانن‌ایاپ یلعف یاهب‌اختنا دیاب            
                                                                                
   ،دناه‌دش داهنشیپ Phase 1/2/3 رد هکنیا لیلد هب طقف ار یلبق یاهم‌یمصت زا مادکچ‌یه 
  .نکن ضرف تسرد                                                                 
                                                                                
   تروصه‌ب Net3 زا و نکن عافد یلبق یریگه‌جیتن زا **هکبش باختنا** یه‌رابرد صوصخه‌ب   
  .نکن تیامح ضرفش‌یپ                                                             
                                                                                
  :دوش صخشم رگید راب کی ،شیامزآ یارجا و دک نتشون زا لبق هک تسا نیا زاف نیا فده  
                                                                                
  ؟تسا مادک اًعقاو همانن‌ایاپ یارب هکبش نیرتهب .1                                 
   لثم دیدج یازجا و درک هدافتسا هیاپ ناونعه‌ب Negm یه‌کبش نامه زا ناوتی‌م ایآ .2   
  ؟درک هفاضا PRV و پمپ                                                          
  ؟دنکی‌م بارخ ار benchmark ای تسا عافدل‌باق و یملع یرییغت نینچ ایآ .3            
  ؟تسا رتهب یرگید یمومع یه‌کبش ای تسا باختنا نیرتهب اًعقاو Net3 ایآ .4            
  ؟دناع‌افدل‌باق و زاجم یتاحالصا هچ اًقیقد ،مینک حالصا ار یاه‌کبش دشاب رارق رگا .5  
   RQ، novelty، fairness، reproducibility، computation رب یرثا هچ باختنا نیا .6 
  ؟دراد شیامزآ یحارط و                                                          
   دراو نآ ساسا رب مناوتب هک هدب **ارجا لباق و نشور میمصت کی** ،تیاهن رد .7     
  implementation موش.

  # CONTEXT هژورپ                                                               
                                                                                
  Deep زا هدافتسا اب Water Distribution Network رد راشف لرتنک یه‌رابرد همانن‌ایاپ 
  Reinforcement Learning تسا.                                                   
                                                                                
  :شور یه‌تسه                                                                    
                                                                                
  * EPANET                                                                      
  * WNTR                                                                        
  * PPO                                                                         
  * continuous action space                                                     
  * PRV control                                                                 
  * Variable-Speed Pump (VSP)                                                   
  * leakage minimization                                                        
  * pressure/service constraints                                                
  * stochastic demand                                                           
  * uncertainty/generalization                                                  
  * multi-objective reward                                                      
  RL و کیسالک یاهbaseline اب هسیاقم *                                           
                                                                                
  Research Question تسا هدیا نیا لوح اًبیرقت یلصا:                               
                                                                                
  ،نئمطمان و ریغتم یاضاقت طیارش رد ،پمپ و PRV گنهامه لرتنک اب دناوتی‌م PPO ایآ > 
   pressure/سیورس تیفیک هکنیا نودب ،دهد شهاک ار leakage و هدرک میظنت ار راشف    
  constraints ؟دنوش ضقن لوبقل‌باقریغ لکش هب                                      
                                                                                
  Proposal دنچ innovation زا یرایسب هک هدش صخشم تایبدا رورم زا دعب اما ،دراد    
  .دناه‌دوب دح زا شیب novelty هیلوا یاهاعدا                                      
                                                                                
  :دوش فیرعت ریز یاهاعدا اب دیابن novelty نیاربانب                              
                                                                                
  * continuous action راب نیلوا یارب                                            
  * EPANET + PPO راب نیلوا یارب                                                 
  * PRV + pump coordination راب نیلوا یارب                                      
  * multi-objective reward راب نیلوا یارب                                       
                                                                                
  .تسا هدش ادیپ اهاعدا نیا هیلع یدهاوش نوچ                                      
                                                                                
  ،attribution، observability ،شیامزآ یحارط** رد رتشیب یلامتحا یروآون ،نآ یاجه‌ب 
  PDA/DDA، uncertainty separation، Pareto evaluation و robustness analysis**    
  .دراد رارق

  **؟مینک باختنا ار یرگید
